I–V)**, each *quaeritur de hoc quod dicit* + a lemma from Lombard's text:

(prior, was 2026-06-03 (Vol III **d10-a2-q3 DONE, commits 650fc31 [chunk+content+paraphrase-audit] + this resume** — DISTINCTIO X, ARTICULUS II (*De filiatione adoptionis*), QUAESTIO III *Utrum filiatio adoptionis sit in nobis per comparationem ad Christum* (whether the sonship of adoption is in us by comparison to Christ — the LAST of Art. II's three inquiries, "are we Christ's own adopted sons?"). **Alignment VERIFIED** vs raw + 450 dpi PDF column bands (opener raw 16840 *Tertio quaeritur, utrum filiatio adoptionis sit in nobis per comparationem ad Christum; et hoc est quaerere, utrum simus filii adoptivi ipsius Christi*; NOT q1 *…sit in Christo* [16508] or q2 *…conveniat nobis per Christum* [16706]). `type: quaestio`, **`has_scholion: FALSE`** — q3 prints NO free-standing scholion; it is covered by q2's scholion §II (forward-covers q3), confirmed by p.238 n.9 *Vide scholion ad praecedentem quaest.* Printed **pp.237–238** (PDF 259–260; raw 16836–16922), offset pdf = printed + 22. **CONCLUSION:** *Filiatio per adoptionem competit nobis respectu totius Trinitatis, et sic etiam Christi* — adoption belongs to us in respect of the whole Trinity (which creates+re-creates us), hence also of Christ, who *can be called our father*: just as the Trinity is one *principium* of nature, so it is one *pater* by the conferral of grace; *pater* said of a Person = personal/notional, said of the rational creature = essential. Replies: (1) the "only the begetter adopts" rule holds of those who beget by generation, but God forms many sons by creation; (2) we and Christ are sons of the same Father but in different modes (Christ per generation = personal act; we per creation+gratification = essential act communicated to the whole Trinity); paternity is *appropriated* to the Father's Person; (3) communicating a property induces confusion only if it is *numbered* (two sons in the Trinity), which would require another Person's incarnation — not the case; (4) the Son of God is our *father* (divine nature) and our *brother* (human nature) — disparate relations in diverse natures, no confusion. **15-entry apparatus**, per-page restart, page-suffixed markers: **p.237 nn.4–9** (`[^p237-4]`–`[^p237-9]`, q3's share; q2 held p.237 nn.1–3), **p.238 nn.1–9** (`[^p238-1]`–`[^p238-9]`, all q3's body). **Footer RECEIVED from d10-a2-q2:** p.237 nn.1–3 consumed by q2; q3 leads with p.237 n.4 (*Matth. 9, 15…*), confirmed eyes-on `/tmp/colcrop/vol3-p237-{L,R}-2.png`. **FORWARDED to d10-dubia: NONE** — q3's footers exhausted at p.238 n.9; the DUBIA block opens on the bottom of p.238 R but its own footer sequence restarts on p.239. **NEXT CHUNK: `bon-sent-III-d10-dubia`** — DUBIA CIRCA LITTERAM MAGISTRI, opener raw **16923** (*In parte ista sunt quaestiones circa litteram, et primo quaeritur de illa distinctione, quam ponit Magister: Secundum aliquando exprimit conditionem naturae…*); **5 dubia (Dub. **TRACTATIO QUAESTIONUM — Article I's 5 quaestiones (ex parte assumentis):**
- q.I — *utrum assumere sit actus conveniens divinae naturae* (whether to assume is an act befitting the divine nature)
- q.II — *utrum sit actus conveniens divinae personae* (…befitting the divine person)
- q.III — *utrum conveniat personae per se, an ratione naturae* (…befits the person in itself, or by reason of the nature)
- q.IV — *utrum conveniat naturae, abstracta omni persona* (…befits the nature, with every person abstracted)
- q.V — *utrum haec sit concedenda: divina natura est caro facta* (whether "the divine nature is made flesh" is to be conceded)

(Article II — *ex parte assumti* — is announced in the TRACTATIO but its own 5-question sub-list appears later, in Article II's territory.)

## ✅ VOL III d3-p2-dubia DONE 2026-06-02 (commit 9214b01) — DUBIA CIRCA LITTERAM MAGISTRI (Dub. I–IV) — **DISTINCTIO III COMPLETE**

`DUBIA CIRCA LITTERAM MAGISTRI.` (PARS II dubia block). **FOUR dubia, Dub. I–IV** — verified from 450 dpi column bands (the resume/brief said THREE; corrected). Latin re-set column-by-column from crops pp.94–95 (raw **7095–7206**), literal English doubt-by-doubt, **15-entry apparatus**, **`has_scholion: false`** (`type: dubia`). Printed **pp.94–95** (PDF 116–117; offset +22). Running heads: p.94 `94 SENTENTIARUM LIB. III.` (dubia block opens bottom of p.94, under the gutter-spanning `DUBIA CIRCA LITTERAM MAGISTRI.`); p.95 `DIST. III. P. II. DUBIA. 95`.
- **Dub. I** (*caro illa… fuit peccato obnoxia in Maria* — answer: non ratione culpae sed foeditatis [culpae causa] + passibilitatis [culpae poena]; both ways before sanctification, the second way after). p.94 L→R.
- **Dub. II** (*primitiam nostrae massae… Christus assumsisse* — triplex ratio: ordo dignitatis [caro excedit dignitatem] / conformitas qualitatis [caruit vitii corruptela] / conformitas virtutis [intensior naturae vigor, primi fructus offerendi Domino]; the argument proceeds *de primitia a prioritate originis*). p.94 R → p.95 L.
- **Dub. III** (*Sola illius non est caro peccati, quia non eum mater concupiscentia, sed gratia concepit* — two readings: (1) *concupiscentia* nominative/appositive [concupiscence = mother in generatione filiorum peccati, Ezech 16 *Pater Amorrhaeus, mater Cethaea*]; (2) better: *concupiscentia/gratia* ablative of concomitance, *mater* = Virgo Maria conceiving with grace, not concupiscence, accompanying). p.95 L→R.
- **Dub. IV** (*Totum Verbum incarnatum est* — *totum* privative [no parts to incarnate] vs positive [ex partibus constitutum / perfectum]; signifies *totalitatem perfectionis* + *privationem partis et partis*, not integritatis; better treated in I Sent. d.19). p.95 R, completes there.
Marginal labels (*Triplex ratio, Distinctio, Ratio 1/2/3, Duplex distinctio, Interpretatio 1, Interpretatio 2. et melior, Subdistinctio*) rendered inline in parens, NOT footnote anchors.

**Apparatus split (15 entries, continuous `[^1]`–`[^15]`, per-page restart).** **p.94 footers 7–10 → `[^1]`–`[^4]`** (INHERITED: p.94 f.1–6 were a3-q2's `[^15]`–`[^20]`, commit dc58438): f.7 *De quo vide supra p.1 a.2 q.2; ante animata vs ante nata* (Dub. I `sanctificata⁷`) / f.8 LONG *Part.I a.2 q.1; II Sent. d.31 a.2 q.1 « In carne Adae duplex corruptio — purae poenalitatis → passibilitas / vitiositatis → culpa originalis » + fomes concupiscentiae* (Dub. I `praecedentibus⁸`) / f.9 *postea: codd. K Z post sanctificationem; S.Thom./Petr.a Tar./Richard./Aegid.* (Dub. I `postea⁹`) / f.10 *Aristot. de Praedicam. de Priori + V Metaph.; edd. primitiva pro primitia* (Dub. II `dignitatis¹⁰`). **p.95 f.1–11 → `[^5]`–`[^15]`**: f.1 deification/Damasc. Marginal labels (*Ad oppositum, Distinctio circa carnem, Conclusio 1–4, Notandum, Propositiones 3 impropriae, Nota, Conclusio principalis, Ratio duplex, Solutio oppositorum, Quid sit ratio seminalis*) rendered inline, NOT footnote anchors.

## Standard per-chunk recipe (Vol II)

See project `CLAUDE.md` → "VOL II OVERRIDE" + "Efficient single-chunk subagent dispatch". In brief: PDF-priority inversion (450 dpi column bands authoritative for Respondeo/Solutio/footers; OCR base for clean prose + marker spacing); `extract-pages.py --volume vol2 --dpi 450` then `colcrop.py vol2 <page>`; backup → re-set Latin column-by-column → literal English → full per-page-restart apparatus → frontmatter Tier-2 + `## Notes` → three `--volume 2` audits + smoke build → two commits (chunk+content.json; then resume).
